## Combo 3617-7715-7722
Cards: Thor, God of Thunder + Time Walk + The Mighty Thor, Jane Foster
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Banned | Popularity: 0 | Commander: not legal | Mana needed: {1}{U} each turn
Produces: Infinite turns; Lock
Start: Thor, God of Thunder (battlefield); Time Walk (hand); The Mighty Thor, Jane Foster (battlefield)
Prerequisites: The Mighty Thor doesn't have summoning sickness.
Steps: Cast Time Walk by paying its mana cost.
  Thor's second ability triggers, dealing damage to any target.
  Resolve Time Walk, causing you to take an extra turn after this one
  Blink Thor using The Mighty Thor.
  When Thor enters, it triggers, exiling Time Walk from your graveyard and allowing you to cast it from exile until the end of your next turn.
  Move to your next turn.
  Cast Time Walk from exile by paying its mana cost.
  Repeat from step 2.

## Combo 362-7916-7924
Cards: Flitwing, Lyev Detective + Ekthi, Contaminator Priest + Bludgeon Brawl
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Banned | Popularity: 0 | Commander: not legal
Produces: Infinite Clue tokens; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact tokens
Start: Flitwing, Lyev Detective (battlefield); Ekthi, Contaminator Priest (battlefield); Bludgeon Brawl (hand)
Prerequisites: You have a way to cause a noncreature artifact to enter under your control.
Steps: Cause a noncreature artifact to enter under your control.
  When the artifact enters as an Equipment, either because it is an Equipment or due to Bludgeon Brawl, its living weapon ability given by it to Ekthi triggers, creating a Clue token due to Flitwing.
  Repeat step 2.

## Combo 372-1309-7339
Cards: Abdel Adrian, Gorion's Ward + Salvager of Secrets + Go Ninja Go
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {R}{W}
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite storm count
Start: Abdel Adrian, Gorion's Ward (battlefield); Salvager of Secrets (exile: exiled by Abdel Adrian); Go Ninja Go (hand)
Prerequisites: You have any number of other nonland permanents that enter the battlefield untapped, and that can collectively tap to produce enough mana to cast Go Ninja Go exiled by Abdel Adrian.
Steps: Cast Go Ninja Go by paying its mana cost, blinking Abdel Adrian.
  Abdel Adrian leaves the battlefield, returning Salvager of Secrets and all other cards that were exiled with it to the battlefield.
  Abdel Adrian and Salvager of Secrets trigger.
  Resolve the Salvager of Secrets trigger, returning Go Ninja Go from your graveyard to your hand.
  Holding priority, activate any number of nonland permanents you control by tapping them, adding at least enough mana to cast Go Ninja Go.
  Resolve the Abdel Adrian trigger, exiling Salvager of Secrets and the mana-producing permanents until Abdel Adrian leaves the battlefield, and creating a 1/1 Soldier creature token for each permanent exiled this way.
  Repeat.

  Holding priority, activate any number of nonland permanents you control by tapping them, adding at least enough mana to cast Splash Portal.
  Resolve the Abdel Adrian trigger, exiling Izzet Chronarch and the mana-producing permanents until Abdel Adrian leaves the battlefield, and creating a 1/1 Soldier creature token for each permanent exiled this way.
  Repeat.

  Blink Thor using Venser, the Sojourner.
  When Thor enters, it triggers, exiling Walk the Aeons from your graveyard and allowing you to cast it from exile until the end of your next turn.
  Move to your next turn.
  Cast Walk the Aeons from exile by paying its mana cost.
  Repeat from step 2.

## Combo 4311-5344-6349
Cards: Charismatic Conqueror + The Twelfth Doctor + Future Sight
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {1}{W}
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite creature ETB for target opponent; Infinite creature tokens for target opponent
Start: Charismatic Conqueror (library: on top of your library); The Twelfth Doctor (battlefield); Future Sight (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have not cast a spell from anywhere but your hand this turn.
Steps: Cast Charismatic Conqueror from the top of your library by paying {1}{W}.
  Charismatic Conqueror's demonstrate ability triggers, causing you and an opponent to copy it.
  Resolve the opponent's copy of Charismatic Conqueror, and then resolve your copy of Charismatic Conqueror.
  When your Charismatic Conqueror token enters the battlefield,  the opponent's Charismatic Conqueror triggers, causing you to tap your Charismatic Conqueror token.
  Resolve the original Charismatic Conqueror spell.
  When the nontoken Charismatic Conqueror enters the battlefield, the opponent's Charismatic Conqueror triggers, causing you to not tap the nontoken Charismatic Conqueror, and causing the opponent to create a 1/1 Vampire creature token.
  When the opponent's Vampire token enters the battlefield, both Charismatic Conquerors you control trigger, causing you to create at least one 1/1 Vampire creature token.
  When your Vampire token enters the battlefield, the opponent's Charismatic Conqueror triggers, causing you to not tap the Vampire token, and causing the opponent to create a 1/1 Vampire creature token.
  Repeat from step 7.

Start: Turnabout (hand); Ghostly Flicker (hand); Warden of the Eye (battlefield); Zealous Lorecaster (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {4}{U}{U}{U}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands you control by tapping them, adding at least {4}{U}{U}{U}.
  Cast Turnabout by paying {2}{U}{U}, untapping all lands you control.
  Cast Ghostly Flicker by paying {2}{U}, blinking Warden of the Eye and Zealous Lorecaster.
  When Warden of the Eye and Zealous Lorecaster enter the battlefield, they trigger, returning Turnabout and Ghostly Flicker from your graveyard to your hand.
  Repeat.
Notes: If lands you control can tap to produce at least {5}{U}{U}{U}, this combo nets infinite mana. You can then stop casting Turnabout and blink one other permanent using Ghostly Flicker. You may also tap artifacts and/or creatures instead for mana if desired.

  Repeat.

  Repeat from step 6.

Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite storm count; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite magecraft triggers
Start: Abdel Adrian, Gorion's Ward (battlefield); Warden of the Eye (exile: exiled by Abdel Adrian); Siren's Ruse (hand)

  Resolve the Warden of the Eye trigger, returning Siren's Ruse from your graveyard to your hand.
  Holding priority, activate any number of nonland permanents you control by tapping them, adding at least enough mana to cast Siren's Ruse.
  Resolve the Abdel Adrian trigger, exiling Warden of the Eye and the mana-producing permanents until Abdel Adrian leaves the battlefield, and creating a 1/1 Soldier creature token for each permanent exiled this way.
  Repeat.

## Combo 719-5331-7082

Start: Sokka and Suki (battlefield); Gemcutter Buccaneer (hand); Xenograft (battlefield: with "Pirate" chosen)

## Combo 719-969-3259-4302
Cards: Bishop of Wings + Breath of Fury + Fervor + Xenograft
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite lifegain; Infinite lifegain triggers; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Start: Bishop of Wings (battlefield); Breath of Fury (battlefield); Fervor (battlefield); Xenograft (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Breath of Fury attached to any other creature you control.
  You control at least one additional creature.
  An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
  "Angel" chosen with Xenograft.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached.
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to any other creature you control, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  When the creature that had Breath of Fury attached dies as an Angel due to Xenograft, Bishop of Wings' second ability triggers, creating a 1/1 Spirit creature token.
  When the Spirit enters the battlefield as an Angel due to Xenograft, Bishop of Wings' first ability triggers, causing you to gain 4 life.
  Repeat each combat phase.

## Combo 719-969-3742-4251-4344

Color identity: URW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal

  The token Lumbering Battlement leaves the battlefield, returning Godo from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat each combat phase.

  Let the Identity Thief token die to the legend rule.
  Repeat from step 3 each turn.

## Combo 799-1750-1979-5261
Cards: Efreet Flamepainter + Isochron Scepter + Pull from Eternity + Karn's Temporal Sundering
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {2} each turn
Produces: Infinite turns; Lock
Start: Efreet Flamepainter (battlefield); Isochron Scepter (battlefield); Pull from Eternity (exile: exiled by Isochron Scepter); Karn's Temporal Sundering (graveyard)
Prerequisites: You control at least one legendary creature and/or planeswalker.
  Efreet Flamepainter does not have summoning sickness and cannot be blocked by an opponent.
Steps: Deal combat damage to an opponent using Efreet Flamepainter.
  Efreet Flamepainter triggers, causing you to cast Karn's Temporal Sundering from your graveyard without paying its mana cost.
  Resolve Karn's Temporal Sundering, causing you to take an extra turn after this one and exile it.
  Activate Isochron Scepter by paying {2} and tapping it, casting a copy of Pull from Eternity without paying its mana cost.
  Resolve Pull from Eternity, putting Karn's Temporal Sundering from exile into your graveyard.
  Repeat each turn.

## Combo 799-1750-4903-5261
Cards: Isochron Scepter + Pull from Eternity + Backdraft Hellkite + Karn's Temporal Sundering
Color identity: URW | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {6}{U}{U}
Produces: Infinite turns; Lock
Start: Isochron Scepter (battlefield); Pull from Eternity (exile: exiled by Isochron Scepter); Backdraft Hellkite (battlefield); Karn's Temporal Sundering (hand)
Prerequisites: Any legendary creature or planeswalker under your control.
Steps: Cast the extra turn spell and move to your extra turn.
  Activate Isochron Sceptre to cast Pull from Eternity, putting the extra turn spell from exile to your graveyard.
  Attack with Backdraft Hellkite to give your extra turn flashback, then cast it.
  Repeat for infinite turns.

## Combo 802-914-1235-2649

Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite artifact tokens; Cast a subset of spells in your graveyard; Infinite colored mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite storm count; Infinite Treasure tokens; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite untap of lands you control
Start: Vadrok, Apex of Thunder (hand, command zone); Dockside Extortionist (battlefield); Twinflame (graveyard); Snap (hand)
Prerequisites: Your opponents control at least three artifacts and/or enchantments.
Steps: Cast Vadrok for its mutate cost by paying {1}{W/U}{R}{R}, merging it under Dockside Extortionist.
  The Dockside Extortionist mutated creature's ability from Vadrok triggers, casting Twinflame from your graveyard without paying its mana cost.
  Resolve Twinflame, creating a token copy of the Dockside Extortionist mutated creature with haste.
  The token copy of the Dockside Extortionist mutated creature triggers, creating at least three Treasure tokens.

You cannot lose the game due to this damage due to The Book of Exalted Deeds, and Karplusan Minotaur cannot be destroyed due to its indestructibility or protection.
  Repeat from step 3.
